.. _class_NetwMultiplayer:

NetwMultiplayer
===============

**Inherits:** :godot:`MultiplayerAPIExtension`

The :godot:`MultiplayerAPI` implementation for a Networked session.

.. rst-class:: classref-introduction-group

Description
-----------

Use :ref:`Netw.of()<class_Netw_method_of>` to get the session for a node. Most game code should use :ref:`Netw<class_Netw>` and the handle classes instead of calling this class directly.

::

    var api := Netw.of(self)
    api.entity_despawn(route)
    api.scene_watch(scene, peer_id)

\ Each method prefix matches a class that wraps it.

- ``scene_*``: :ref:`NetwSceneHandle<class_NetwSceneHandle>`\ 

- ``interest_*``: :ref:`NetwInterestHandle<class_NetwInterestHandle>`\ 

- ``session_*``: :ref:`NetwSessionHandle<class_NetwSessionHandle>`\ 

- ``clock_*``: :ref:`NetwClockHandle<class_NetwClockHandle>`\ 

- ``predict_*``: :ref:`NetwPredictionHandle<class_NetwPredictionHandle>`\ 

- ``entity_*``, ``liveness_*``, and ``spawn_*``: :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\ 

- ``table_*``: :ref:`NetwTableHandle<class_NetwTableHandle>`\ 

- ``schema_*``: :ref:`NetwSchema<class_NetwSchema>`\ 

- ``lagcomp_*``: :ref:`NetwAction<class_NetwAction>`\ 

- ``peer_*``: :ref:`NetwPlayer<class_NetwPlayer>`\ 

- ``display_*``: :ref:`NetwDisplayHandle<class_NetwDisplayHandle>`\ 

- ``endpoint_*`` and ``embed_*``: :ref:`NetwConnectHandle<class_NetwConnectHandle>`\ 

- ``transport_*``: :ref:`NetwTransport<class_NetwTransport>`\ 

- ``rpc_*`` and ``sync_*``: :ref:`NetwMemberConfig<class_NetwMemberConfig>`\ 

- ``property_set_*``: :ref:`NetwPropertySet<class_NetwPropertySet>`\ 

- ``service_*``: :ref:`NetwService<class_NetwService>`\ 

- ``database_*`` and ``persist_*``: :ref:`NetwDatabase<class_NetwDatabase>` and :ref:`NetwPersistenceHandle<class_NetwPersistenceHandle>`\ 

- ``stats_*`` and ``attribution_*``: :ref:`NetwPredictStats<class_NetwPredictStats>`\ 

Methods beginning with an underscore are virtual. Override one in a script and call the matching ``_default`` method to run the default behavior.

::

    extends NetwMultiplayer

    func _predict_consume(
            depth: int, buffer: int) -> NetwPredict.ConsumeAction:
        if depth > 8:
            return NetwPredict.CONSUME_ACTION_REPLAY
        return predict_consume_default(depth, buffer)

\ Install the subclass with :ref:`MultiplayerTree.api_script<class_MultiplayerTree_property_api_script>`. ``super`` does not work on these methods, so call the ``_default`` method.

Signals
-------

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_after_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick>`

Emitted at the end of each tick, after :ref:`clock_on_tick<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_on_tick>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick_loop:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_after_tick_loop**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick_loop>`

Emitted once per physics frame, after that frame's ticks, even when there were none. :ref:`clock_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>` does not emit it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_before_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick>`

Emitted at the start of each tick, before :ref:`clock_on_tick<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_on_tick>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick_loop:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_before_tick_loop**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick_loop>`

Emitted once per physics frame, before that frame's ticks, even when there are none. :ref:`clock_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>` does not emit it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_configured:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_configured**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_configured>`

Emitted once when the clock settings are applied. Check :ref:`clock_is_configured()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_configured>` in case it already happened.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_on_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_on_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_on_tick>`

Emitted every tick. Run game logic here.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_pong_received:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_pong_received**\ (\ data\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_pong_received>`

Emitted when the server answers a :ref:`clock_send_ping()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_send_ping>` and the clock has adjusted.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴rtt_raw                     float   this round trip, in seconds
    ┠╴rtt_avg                     float   average round trip, in seconds
    ┠╴rtt_jitter                  float   round trip variation, in seconds
    ┠╴diff                        int     ticks the clock moved
    ┠╴tick                        int     the tick after adjusting
    ┠╴display_offset              float   the current display offset
    ┠╴recommended_display_offset  float   the display offset this ping suggests
    ┠╴is_stable                   bool    clock_is_stable()
    ┖╴is_synchronized             bool    clock_is_synchronized()

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_stability_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_stability_changed**\ (\ is_stable\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_stability_changed>`

Emitted when :ref:`clock_is_stable()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_stable>` changes.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_synchronized:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_synchronized**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_synchronized>`

Emitted once, the first time the clock synchronizes with the server.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_tickrate_mismatch:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**clock_tickrate_mismatch**\ (\ peer_id\: :godot:`int`, their_tickrate\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_tickrate_mismatch>`

Emitted when ``peer_id`` runs ``their_tickrate``, which differs from this peer's, and :ref:`clock_set_mismatch_action()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_mismatch_action>` is :ref:`MISMATCH_ACTION_SIGNAL<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_SIGNAL>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Emitted when an entity fails to load or save in ``database``. ``detail`` names the record and the reason.

**endpoint_removed**\ (\ endpoint\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_removed>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_updated:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**endpoint_updated**\ (\ endpoint\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_updated>`

Emitted when a probe updates ``endpoint``. Read it with :ref:`endpoint_get_param()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_get_param>` and :ref:`endpoint_get_state()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_get_state>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_dead**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_dead>`

Emitted once when the entity at ``route`` is despawned, after :ref:`NetwEntity.despawned<class_NetwEntity_signal_despawned>`. A peer that only stops seeing the entity emits :ref:`entity_hidden<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_hidden**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_hidden>`

Emitted when this peer stops seeing ``entity``, which still exists on the server. If it becomes visible again, the same route gets a new node. Emitted after :ref:`NetwEntity.hidden<class_NetwEntity_signal_hidden>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_lingering:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_lingering**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_lingering>`

Emitted when an entity starts despawning and enters :ref:`ENTITY_STATE_LINGERING<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENTITY_STATE_LINGERING>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_live:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**entity_live**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_entity_live>`

Emitted when ``entity`` spawns on this peer at ``route``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_lagcomp_action_gate_fallback:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**lagcomp_action_gate_fallback**\ (\ key\: :godot:`StringName`, view_tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_lagcomp_action_gate_fallback>`

Emitted when a :ref:`NetwAction.TIMING_TICK_ALIGNED_STATE_READY<class_NetwAction_constant_TIMING_TICK_ALIGNED_STATE_READY>` action runs without all its input, after waiting :ref:`NetwLagCompensationConfig.input_gate_deadline_ticks<class_NetwLagCompensationConfig_property_input_gate_deadline_ticks>`. ``view_tick`` is the tick it was aimed at.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

Emitted when :ref:`scene_player_viewport()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_player_viewport>` changes. ``viewport`` may be ``null``.

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_persist_saved:

----

----

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_poll_started:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_poll_started**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_poll_started>`

Emitted at the start of each :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.poll() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_poll>`. ``delta`` is the time since the previous one.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_reclaimed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_reclaimed**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_reclaimed>`

Emitted right after :ref:`session_ended<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_ended>`. Free nodes the session owned here.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_server_disconnecting:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_server_disconnecting**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_server_disconnecting>`

Emitted when the server calls :ref:`session_notify_shutdown()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_notify_shutdown>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_state_changed:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_state_changed**\ (\ old_state\: :ref:`SessionState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SessionState>`, new_state\: :ref:`SessionState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SessionState>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_state_changed>`

Emitted when :ref:`state<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_state>` changes.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_paused:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_tree_paused**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_paused>`

Emitted when the server calls :ref:`session_pause()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_pause>`. It does not pause the :godot:`SceneTree`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_unpaused:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**session_tree_unpaused**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_unpaused>`

Emitted when the server calls :ref:`session_unpause()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_unpause>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_table_received:

.. rst-class:: classref-signal

**table_received**\ (\ table\: :godot:`RID`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_table_received>`

Emitted when ``table`` changes, by :ref:`table_commit()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_commit>` or by an update from the server.

.. rst-class:: classref-section-separator

Enumerations
------------

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **Event**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWNING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWNING** = ``0``

An entity is about to spawn. ``detail`` is empty.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWNED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWNED** = ``1``

An entity spawned. ``detail`` is empty.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DESPAWNING** = ``2``

An entity started despawning. ``detail`` has ``reason``, and ``model`` has ``entity_id`` and ``peer_id``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DESPAWNED** = ``3``

An entity finished despawning. ``model`` is the one from :ref:`EVENT_DESPAWNING<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DESPAWNING>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_REPARENTED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_REPARENTED** = ``4``

An entity was reparented. ``detail`` has ``reason`` and ``moved``, which is ``true`` when its position changed too.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_STAGE_TRANSITION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_STAGE_TRANSITION** = ``5``

An entity's :ref:`Stage<enum_NetwEntity_Stage>` changed. ``detail`` has ``from`` and ``to``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_CONTROL_CHANGED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_CONTROL_CHANGED** = ``6``

The peer controlling an entity changed. ``detail`` has ``from`` and ``to``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_CONTROL_REQUESTED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_CONTROL_REQUESTED** = ``7``

A peer asked to control an entity. ``detail`` has ``requester``, and ``verdict`` is :godot:`@GlobalScope.OK <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_OK>` when granted.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_HIDDEN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_HIDDEN** = ``8``

An entity was hidden without being despawned. ``detail`` is empty.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_INTEREST_ENTER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_INTEREST_ENTER** = ``16``

A peer started seeing an entity through a layer. ``detail`` has ``layer``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_INTEREST_EXIT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_INTEREST_EXIT** = ``17``

A peer stopped seeing an entity through a layer. ``detail`` has ``layer``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_OBSERVER_ENTERED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_OBSERVER_ENTERED** = ``18``

A peer started seeing an entity, along with :ref:`NetwEntity.observer_entered<class_NetwEntity_signal_observer_entered>`. ``detail`` has ``layer``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_OBSERVER_LEFT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_OBSERVER_LEFT** = ``19``

A peer stopped seeing an entity, along with :ref:`NetwEntity.observer_left<class_NetwEntity_signal_observer_left>`. ``detail`` has ``layer``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_INTEREST_COMMIT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_INTEREST_COMMIT** = ``20``

Pending layer changes were applied. ``route`` is ``0``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_EPISODE_OPEN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_EPISODE_OPEN** = ``32``

A run of mispredictions started. ``detail`` has ``transition`` and ``attribution``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_EPISODE_CLOSE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_EPISODE_CLOSE** = ``33``

A run of mispredictions ended. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``attribution`` and how it ended.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_EPISODE_FALLBACK:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_EPISODE_FALLBACK** = ``34``

Prediction gave up on an entity and fell back to the server's state. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``attribution`` and ``demoted``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_DRIVE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_DRIVE** = ``35``

A predicted entity simulated one input. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``label``, ``kind`` and ``fresh``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_CONSUME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_CONSUME** = ``36``

The server used a queued input. ``detail`` has ``depth``, ``buffer`` and ``action``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_EVALUATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_EVALUATE** = ``37``

A prediction was compared with the server's state. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``divergence`` and ``corrected``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_PREDICT_RECOVER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_PREDICT_RECOVER** = ``38``

A misprediction was corrected. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``skip`` and ``teleport``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DIVERGENCE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DIVERGENCE** = ``39``

A prediction differed from the server's state by more than the tolerance, along with :ref:`NetwPredictionHandle.divergence_detected<class_NetwPredictionHandle_signal_divergence_detected>`. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``attribution`` and ``divergence``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_RECOVERY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_RECOVERY** = ``40``

A predicted entity was corrected, along with :ref:`NetwPredictionHandle.recovered<class_NetwPredictionHandle_signal_recovered>`. ``detail`` has ``transition``, ``attribution``, ``teleport`` and ``moved``, the properties that changed.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_SYNC:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_SYNC** = ``48``

An incoming synchronization packet was accepted or rejected by :ref:`_sync_admit_frame()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_admit_frame>`. ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_SPAWN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_SPAWN** = ``49``

An incoming spawn packet was accepted or rejected by :ref:`_spawn_admit_frame()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_admit_frame>`. ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_TABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_TABLE** = ``50``

An incoming table packet was accepted or rejected by :ref:`_table_admit_frame()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__table_admit_frame>`. ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATE_PREDICT:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATE_PREDICT** = ``51``

An incoming prediction packet was accepted or rejected by :ref:`_predict_admit_frame()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_admit_frame>`. ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SYNC_ENCODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SYNC_ENCODE** = ``64``

A synchronization packet was written for ``peer``. ``detail`` has ``bytes``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SYNC_DECODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SYNC_DECODE** = ``65``

A synchronization packet was read. ``detail`` has ``comp``, and ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_GATHER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_GATHER** = ``66``

Changed properties were collected for sending. ``detail`` has ``offers`` and ``sends``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_APPLY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_APPLY** = ``67``

Received packets from ``peer`` were applied. ``detail`` has ``frames``, and ``verdict`` is the worst error.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWN_DECLARE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_SPAWN_DECLARE** = ``68``

:ref:`_spawn_declare()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_declare>` ran. ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWN_RECONCILE:

The server decided which entities to spawn on which peers. ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SPAWN_CONSTRUCT:

:ref:`_spawn_construct()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_construct>` ran. ``detail`` has ``built``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DISPLAY_RECORD:

:ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` **EVENT_DISPLAY_RECORD** = ``71``

An interpolated property recorded a value. ``detail`` has ``track`` and ``tick``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DISPLAY_PUMP:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

Interpolation advanced. ``detail`` has ``tick``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_DISPLAY_WRITE:

:ref:`_display_write()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__display_write>` ran. ``detail`` has ``track``, and ``verdict`` is the result.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_TABLE_COMMIT:

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **DisplayRole**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTO** = ``0``

Picked from :ref:`NetwSimulationHandle.mode<class_NetwSimulationHandle_property_mode>`. The default. Predicted entities use :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED>`, received ones :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE>`, ones this peer controls without prediction :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED>`, and the server uses :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE** = ``1``

Interpolate between received states. Used for other players' entities.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_PREDICTED** = ``2``

Follow the locally predicted body, as set by :ref:`NetwDisplayHandle.live_mode<class_NetwDisplayHandle_property_live_mode>`. Used for the player's own entity.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED** = ``3``

No smoothing. The game moves the node itself.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayRole<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayRole>` **DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY** = ``4``

Record the server's own simulation every tick and interpolate it, so the host sees it as smooth as clients do.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration

enum **DisplayPump**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_UNRESOLVED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_UNRESOLVED** = ``-1``

Not decided yet.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_DISABLED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_DISABLED** = ``0``

No interpolation, as with :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_DISABLED>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_REMOTE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_REMOTE** = ``1``

Interpolate received states, as with :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_REMOTE>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_BRACKETED:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_BRACKETED** = ``2``

Interpolate between the previous and current tick, one tick behind. Used by :ref:`DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_ROLE_AUTHORITY>` and :ref:`LIVE_MODE_BRACKETED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LIVE_MODE_BRACKETED>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PUMP_CHASE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayPump<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayPump>` **DISPLAY_PUMP_CHASE** = ``3``

Move the visual toward the body every frame. Used by :ref:`LIVE_MODE_CHASE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LIVE_MODE_CHASE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>` **TIMELINE_MODE_BUFFERED** = ``0``

Display slightly in the past, between received states. The default, and always used on the server.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>` **TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST** = ``1``

Guess ahead from the last received velocity. Less delay, but it ignores collisions, so a body can move into a wall until the next update arrives.

The scene shares the physics and rendering world with the rest of the game.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_ISOLATION_OWN_WORLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam:

enum **ClockParam**: :ref:`🔗<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE** = ``0``

:godot:`int` ticks per second. Must match on every peer, so it can only be set through :ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_SYNC_MODE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_SYNC_MODE** = ``1``

The :ref:`SyncMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SyncMode>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PING_INTERVAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_PING_INTERVAL** = ``2``

:godot:`float` seconds between pings.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME** = ``3``

:godot:`int` most ticks run in one frame. Leftover time carries to the next frame.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD** = ``4``

:godot:`float` seconds. A longer frame is treated as a hitch and skipped.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD** = ``5``

:godot:`int` ticks. Past this difference, :ref:`SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` corrects in one step.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STRETCH_NUDGE_FACTOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_STRETCH_NUDGE_FACTOR** = ``6``

:godot:`float` share of the difference :ref:`SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` corrects each frame.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET** = ``7``

:godot:`int` ticks the display runs behind the simulation, which gives interpolation room.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_LEAD_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_LEAD_TICKS** = ``8``

:godot:`float` ticks a client runs ahead of the server.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER** = ``9``

:godot:`float` weight of jitter in :ref:`clock_get_recommended_display_offset()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_WINDOW** = ``10``

:godot:`int` number of recent pings averaged.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_STABILITY_THRESHOLD:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_STABILITY_THRESHOLD** = ``11``

:godot:`float` seconds of jitter under which :ref:`clock_is_stable()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_stable>` is ``true``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_USE_PHYSICS_INTERPOLATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_USE_PHYSICS_INTERPOLATION** = ``12``

:godot:`bool`. Whether :ref:`clock_get_tick_factor()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor>` follows :godot:`Engine.get_physics_interpolation_fraction() <Engine#class_Engine_method_get_physics_interpolation_fraction>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_ENABLE_DRIFT_LOGGING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_ENABLE_DRIFT_LOGGING** = ``13``

:godot:`bool`. Whether to log how far the clock drifts.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK** = ``14``

:godot:`bool`. When ``true``, ticks only run through :ref:`clock_step()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>` **CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE** = ``15``

:godot:`float`. A fixed value for :ref:`clock_get_tick_factor()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor>`. Negative turns it off.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT:

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG:

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_JITTER:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_JITTER** = ``2``

The round-trip variation, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_ONE_WAY_LATENCY:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_ONE_WAY_LATENCY** = ``3``

Half of :ref:`CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_RTT_AVG>`, in seconds.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICKTIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

Seconds per tick, one divided by :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_TICK_ACCUMULATOR:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

Seconds collected toward the next tick.

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

Physics frames run since the clock started.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_POLLS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`ClockMonitor<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockMonitor>` **CLOCK_MONITOR_POLLS** = ``7``

:godot:`MultiplayerAPI.poll() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_poll>` calls since the clock started.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_WALL_SECONDS:

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_MONITOR_PHYSICS_HZ:

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_LIVE_SMOOTH_TIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_CHASE_GLIDE_TIME:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`TimelineMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TimelineMode>`. How a remote entity is displayed.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_FORECAST_TICKS** = ``5``

:godot:`int` ticks :ref:`TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TIMELINE_MODE_FORECAST>` may guess ahead before it stops.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_SMART_DILATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_SMART_DILATION** = ``6``

:godot:`bool`. Whether playback may slow down to cover late packets.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_MAX_EXTRA_DILATION:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_LAG_ADAPT_RATE:

:godot:`float`. How fast the interpolation delay follows changes in latency. Higher reacts faster but is less smooth.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GROWTH:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_FLOOR_SMOOTHING:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:godot:`float`. How much the smallest interpolation delay is smoothed over time.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GRACE_FRAMES:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`DisplayParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_DisplayParam>` **DISPLAY_PARAM_STARVATION_GRACE_FRAMES** = ``11``

:godot:`int` frames with no new state before updates are considered stalled.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_DISPLAY_PARAM_TRACE_INTERVAL:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:godot:`int` ticks between debug traces, or ``0`` for none.

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_TELEPORT_THRESHOLD:

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_DIVERGENCE_EPSILON:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:godot:`float`. How far a prediction may differ from the server before it counts as a misprediction.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_BREACH_RESPONSE:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_COLLISION_COOLDOWN_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:godot:`int` ticks after a collision during which corrections replace every property.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_CONSUME_PER_TICK:

:godot:`int`. The most queued inputs the server runs in one tick.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_MAX_CONSUME_LAG_TICKS:

:godot:`int` ticks the server may fall behind a client's inputs before it skips ahead.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_CONSUME_BUFFER_TICKS:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:godot:`int` inputs the server keeps queued before running one, the ``buffer`` of :ref:`predict_consume()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_consume>`. ``0`` by default.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PREDICT_PARAM_REPLAY_BUFFER_DEPTH:

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ATTRIBUTED_FRAMES_IN:

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

:ref:`Stat<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Stat>` **STAT_ATTRIBUTED_FRAMES_IN** = ``94``

Counter. Messages received, counted in :ref:`attribution_snapshot()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_snapshot>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-enumeration-constant

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_STAT_ATTRIBUTION_DROPPED_OUT:

Constants
---------

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-constant

**CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE** = ``88`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE>`

The first byte of a reliable Networked packet. Packets sent with :ref:`send_bytes()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_bytes>` must not start with this byte, :ref:`CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE>` or :ref:`CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE_ACKED<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE_ACKED>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE:

.. rst-class:: classref-constant

**CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE** = ``120`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE>`

The first byte of an unreliable Networked packet. See :ref:`CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE_ACKED:

.. rst-class:: classref-constant

**CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE_ACKED** = ``152`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_UNRELIABLE_ACKED>`

The first byte of an unreliable Networked packet that also acknowledges received packets. See :ref:`CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CARRIER_MAGIC_RELIABLE>`.

Every player, sorted by peer id. Same as :ref:`players<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_players>`, except that a player who is leaving stays here a little longer.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner:

The :godot:`SceneMultiplayer` this session wraps. It owns the :godot:`MultiplayerPeer`, the connection and authentication. Never ``null``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_host:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:godot:`bool` **is_host** = ``true`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_host>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`bool` **get_is_host**\ (\ )

``true`` on a server, and when not connected at all, as with :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.is_server() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_is_server>`. ``false`` on a client, even before it connects. Use :ref:`is_online<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_online>` to also require a connection.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_local_client:

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :godot:`bool` **get_is_local_client**\ (\ )

``true`` when this peer has its own player, which is at :ref:`ROLE_CLIENT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ROLE_CLIENT>` and :ref:`ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

``true`` when :ref:`state<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_state>` is :ref:`SESSION_STATE_ONLINE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SESSION_STATE_ONLINE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

The peer that sent the RPC being run, like :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.get_remote_sender_id() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_get_remote_sender_id>`.

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_role:

.. rst-class:: classref-property

:ref:`Role<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Role>` **role** = ``0`` :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_role>`

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

- :ref:`Role<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Role>` **session_get_role**\ (\ )

What this peer is in the session. :ref:`is_host<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_host>` and :ref:`is_local_client<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_is_local_client>` are usually what a game wants.

- :ref:`ROLE_NONE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ROLE_NONE>` no session

- :ref:`ROLE_CLIENT<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ROLE_CLIENT>` a client

- :ref:`ROLE_DEDICATED_SERVER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ROLE_DEDICATED_SERVER>` a server with no player

- :ref:`ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ROLE_LISTEN_SERVER>` a server that also plays

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_property_state:

.. rst-class:: classref-property-setget

Whether the session is connected.

- :ref:`SESSION_STATE_OFFLINE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SESSION_STATE_OFFLINE>` not connected

- :ref:`SESSION_STATE_CONNECTING<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SESSION_STATE_CONNECTING>` connecting, may fail

Method Descriptions
-------------------

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__display_write:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_display_write**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, track\: :godot:`StringName`, value\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__display_write>`

Writes one display ``track`` for ``entity``. With no override this returns :ref:`display_write_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_write_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__persist_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **_persist_tick**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__persist_tick>`

Saves persistent entities. :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.poll() <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_method_poll>` calls it with ``delta``, the seconds since the last poll. Override it to decide when to save, and call :ref:`persist_tick_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_persist_tick_default>` to save.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_admit_frame:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_predict_admit_frame**\ (\ sender\: :godot:`int`, route\: :godot:`int`, channel\: :godot:`int`, payload\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_admit_frame>`

Accepts or rejects an incoming prediction packet. Return :godot:`@GlobalScope.OK <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_OK>` to accept it. With no override this returns :ref:`predict_admit_frame_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_admit_frame_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_consume:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`ConsumeAction<enum_NetwPredict_ConsumeAction>` **_predict_consume**\ (\ depth\: :godot:`int`, buffer\: :godot:`int`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_consume>`

Decides what the authority does with a predicted entity's input queue, called once per tick. ``depth`` is how many inputs are queued and ``buffer`` is how many it should hold. With no override this returns :ref:`predict_consume_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_consume_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_drive:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPredictFold<class_NetwPredictFold>` **_predict_drive**\ (\ latest_input_tick\: :godot:`int`, last_driven_input_tick\: :godot:`int`, frame_tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_drive>`

Picks which input a predicted entity simulates this frame. ``latest_input_tick`` is the newest input it holds, ``last_driven_input_tick`` is the last one it simulated, and ``frame_tick`` is the tick to use when it holds none. With no override this returns :ref:`predict_drive_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_drive_default>`. Returning ``null`` emits :ref:`EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE>` and the default is used.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_evaluate:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPredictJudgement<class_NetwPredictJudgement>` **_predict_evaluate**\ (\ domain\: :ref:`Domain<enum_NetwPredict_Domain>`, verdict\: :ref:`ExactVerdict<enum_NetwPredict_ExactVerdict>`, predicted\: :godot:`Dictionary`, payload\: :godot:`Dictionary`, wiring\: :godot:`Dictionary`, field_sink\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_evaluate>`

Decides whether a predicted state matches the state the authority sent. ``predicted`` and ``payload`` are the two states. With no override this returns :ref:`predict_evaluate_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_evaluate_default>`. Returning ``null`` emits :ref:`EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE>` and the default is used.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_recover:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPredictRecovery<class_NetwPredictRecovery>` **_predict_recover**\ (\ payload\: :godot:`Dictionary`, policy\: :ref:`RecoveryPolicy<enum_NetwPredict_RecoveryPolicy>`, restore\: :ref:`Restore<enum_NetwSimulationHandle_Restore>`, projection\: :godot:`Dictionary`, current\: :godot:`Dictionary`, pose_errors\: :godot:`Dictionary`, wiring\: :godot:`Dictionary`, verdict\: :godot:`Dictionary`, tick_delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__predict_recover>`

Decides how a mispredicted entity is corrected to the authority's state. ``payload`` is the authority's state, ``current`` is the local one, and ``policy`` is :ref:`NetwPredictionHandle.resolved_recovery_policy()<class_NetwPredictionHandle_method_resolved_recovery_policy>`. With no override this returns :ref:`predict_recover_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_predict_recover_default>`. Returning ``null`` emits :ref:`EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EVENT_SEAM_MISUSE>` and the default is used.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_admit_frame:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_spawn_admit_frame**\ (\ sender\: :godot:`int`, route\: :godot:`int`, channel\: :godot:`int`, payload\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_admit_frame>`

Accepts or rejects an incoming spawn packet. Return :godot:`@GlobalScope.OK <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_OK>` to accept it. With no override this returns :ref:`spawn_admit_frame_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_spawn_admit_frame_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_construct:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Node` **_spawn_construct**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_construct>`

Creates the node for an entity the server spawned on this peer. Return the node without adding it to the tree. With no override this returns :ref:`spawn_construct_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_spawn_construct_default>`.

Override it to pool nodes or pick a different scene.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_declare:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_spawn_declare**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, recipe\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_declare>`

Allows ``entity`` to be spawned on other peers. It does not spawn it. ``recipe`` is what a remote peer needs to create the node, and holds plain values only. With no override this returns :ref:`spawn_declare_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_spawn_declare_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_undeclare:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **_spawn_undeclare**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__spawn_undeclare>`

Called when ``entity`` can no longer be spawned on other peers. Copies peers already hold stay. The default does nothing.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_admit_frame:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_sync_admit_frame**\ (\ sender\: :godot:`int`, route\: :godot:`int`, comp\: :godot:`int`, channel\: :godot:`int`, flags\: :godot:`int`, tick\: :godot:`int`, payload\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`\ ) |virtual| |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_admit_frame>`

Accepts or rejects an incoming synchronization packet. Return :godot:`@GlobalScope.OK <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_OK>` to accept it. With no override this returns :ref:`sync_admit_frame_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_sync_admit_frame_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_apply_set:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_sync_apply_set**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`, values\: :godot:`Array`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_apply_set>`

Writes received property ``values`` into ``entity``. With no override this returns :ref:`sync_apply_set_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_sync_apply_set_default>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_decode:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **_sync_decode**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`, flags\: :godot:`int`, tick\: :godot:`int`, payload\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_decode>`

Decodes a received synchronization ``payload`` for ``entity``. It reads what :ref:`_sync_encode()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_encode>` wrote on the sender, so override both together. Return an error to drop the packet without changing the node.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                 the values are applied
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED   no decoder exists for this entity

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_encode:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`PackedByteArray` **_sync_encode**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_encode>`

Returns the synchronization payload sent to ``peer`` on ``tick``. An empty array sends nothing. :ref:`_sync_decode()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_decode>` reads it on the other side, so override both together.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

:godot:`Array` **_sync_gather_set**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`\ ) |virtual| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_gather_set>`

Reads the synchronized property values from ``entity``. With no override this returns :ref:`sync_gather_set_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_sync_gather_set_default>`.

Called when packet ``sequence`` is sent to ``peer``. With no override this calls :ref:`sync_note_sent_default()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_sync_note_sent_default>`. An override that skips the default drops what the packet carried.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_is_armed:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **attribution_is_armed**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_is_armed>`

Returns ``true`` when :ref:`attribution_set_armed()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_set_armed>` turned on per-column counting.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_set_armed:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **attribution_set_armed**\ (\ armed\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_set_armed>`

Turns on counting bits per synchronized property, read from :ref:`attribution_snapshot()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_snapshot>` under ``columns``. The count is each property's declared size, which can be larger than what compression actually sent. It works in release builds.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_snapshot:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **attribution_snapshot**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_attribution_snapshot>`

Returns where this session's bandwidth went.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴bytes_out             Dictionary  peer -> channel -> bytes sent
    ┠╴bytes_in              Dictionary  peer -> channel -> bytes received
    ┠╴route_bytes_out       Dictionary  route -> bytes sent
    ┠╴route_bytes_in        Dictionary  route -> bytes received
    ┠╴attributed_out        int         the sum of bytes_out
    ┠╴attributed_in         int         the sum of bytes_in
    ┠╴framing_out           int         counted as sent, carrying no frame
    ┠╴frames_out            int         frames sent
    ┠╴frames_in             int         frames received
    ┠╴staged_dropped_out    int         bytes encoded and never sent
    ┠╴frames_in_by_channel  Dictionary  peer -> channel -> frames received
    ┠╴refused_in            int         frames rejected on arrival
    ┠╴refusals              Dictionary  peer -> channel -> rejection name -> count
    ┠╴armed                 bool        whether column grain is being recorded
    ┠╴columns               Dictionary  a packed key -> schema, column, bits
    ┖╴residual              Dictionary  the same bytes at the datagram boundary

\ Route ``0`` is traffic that belongs to no entity. ``residual`` counts the same traffic including packet headers, with this shape.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴datagram_bytes_out    int  what sent_bytes holds
    ┠╴attributed_out        int  the sum of bytes_out
    ┠╴framing_out           int  sent bytes carrying no frame
    ┠╴staged_dropped_out    int  bytes encoded and never sent
    ┠╴residual_out          int  sent bytes neither table claims
    ┠╴datagram_bytes_in     int  what received_bytes holds
    ┠╴attributed_in         int  the sum of bytes_in
    ┠╴residual_in           int  the envelopes around those payloads
    ┠╴wire_bytes_out        int  whole datagrams given the transport
    ┠╴wire_bytes_in         int  whole datagrams taken from it
    ┠╴datagrams_out         int  how many of those went out
    ┠╴datagrams_in          int  how many came in
    ┠╴carrier_overhead_out  int  datagram headers written
    ┖╴carrier_overhead_in   int  those headers plus the envelopes

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Forgets every peer and player, including :ref:`local_player<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_local_player>`. Nobody is disconnected. :ref:`peer_forget()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_forget>` does the same for one peer.

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_auto_configure_offset**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_auto_configure_offset>`

Measures the connection for five seconds, then sets :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET>` to the largest value :ref:`clock_get_recommended_display_offset()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset>` returned. Does nothing on the server.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_config:

Returns a copy of the current clock settings. Changing the copy has no effect.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_display_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_display_tick**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_display_tick>`

The tick to render, which is :ref:`clock_get_tick()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick>` minus :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_DISPLAY_OFFSET>`, never below zero.

Returns one clock diagnostic value.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_param:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Variant` **clock_get_param**\ (\ param\: :ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_param>`

Returns the current value of one clock setting, as an :godot:`int`, :godot:`float` or :godot:`bool` depending on the setting.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_factor:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_physics_factor**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_factor>`

Physics frames per tick, unrounded. 60 physics ticks per second with a :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE>` of 30 returns ``2.0``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_steps_per_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_physics_steps_per_tick**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_steps_per_tick>`

:ref:`clock_get_physics_factor()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_physics_factor>` rounded to a whole number, never below one.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_recommended_display_offset**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_recommended_display_offset>`

The display offset this connection needs, in ticks. It is the one-way latency plus the jitter times :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_JITTER_MULTIPLIER>`, rounded up. It is not applied. :ref:`clock_auto_configure_offset()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_auto_configure_offset>` applies it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_simulation_behind_count:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_simulation_behind_count**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_simulation_behind_count>`

How many frames skipped a tick because the clock runs at most one tick per frame. A count that keeps growing means this machine is too slow to keep up with :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **clock_get_tick**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick>`

The current server-calibrated simulation tick.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_tick_factor**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_factor>`

How far time has moved past the last tick, as a fraction of a tick. Use it to interpolate between ticks. :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_TICK_FACTOR_OVERRIDE>` replaces it with a fixed value.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_phase:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`float` **clock_get_tick_phase**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_get_tick_phase>`

How far into the current tick the clock is, from ``0.0`` to ``1.0``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_ingest_pong:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **clock_ingest_pong**\ (\ sample\: :godot:`float`, server_tick_at_pong\: :godot:`int`, server_tick_phase\: :godot:`float`, apply_lead\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_ingest_pong>`

Adjusts the local tick toward the server's using one round-trip ``sample``, in seconds. :ref:`SYNC_MODE_SNAP<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_SNAP>` corrects at once, and :ref:`SYNC_MODE_STRETCH<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SYNC_MODE_STRETCH>` corrects a little each frame until the difference passes :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_PANIC_SNAP_THRESHOLD>`. Returns the same :godot:`Dictionary` :ref:`clock_pong_received<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_pong_received>` carries.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴rtt_raw                     float   this sample's round trip, in seconds
    ┠╴rtt_avg                     float   the running average round trip
    ┠╴rtt_jitter                  float   the running jitter estimate
    ┠╴diff                        int     ticks the calibration moved this peer
    ┠╴tick                        int     the tick after calibrating
    ┠╴display_offset              float   the offset the display is running
    ┠╴recommended_display_offset  float   the offset this sample recommends
    ┠╴is_stable                   bool    whether jitter is under threshold
    ┖╴is_synchronized             bool    whether a calibration has ever landed

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Returns ``true`` when a :ref:`NetwClockConfig<class_NetwClockConfig>` was applied. Without one the tick does not move.

:godot:`bool` **clock_is_synchronized**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_synchronized>`

Returns ``true`` once the clock has synchronized with the server at least once.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_physics_step:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_physics_step**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_physics_step>`

Advances the clock by ``delta`` seconds and runs every tick that completes. The session calls it every physics frame unless :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MANUAL_TICK>` is set. A frame longer than :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_STALL_THRESHOLD>` is skipped, and no frame runs more than :ref:`CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_CLOCK_PARAM_MAX_TICKS_PER_FRAME>` ticks.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_request_handshake:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_request_handshake**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_request_handshake>`

Asks the server for its tickrate. :ref:`clock_set_mismatch_action()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_mismatch_action>` decides what happens when it differs. The session calls this on clients when they connect.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_send_ping:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_send_ping**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_send_ping>`

Sends an unreliable ping to the server. The reply emits :ref:`clock_pong_received<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_pong_received>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_set_gate**\ (\ armed\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_gate>`

While on, physics steps only on frames that have a tick to run, so a tick covers the same physics time on every peer. :ref:`clock_is_simulating()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_simulating>` tells whether this frame steps. Calls nest, so it turns off when every ``true`` is matched by a ``false``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_mismatch_action:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_set_mismatch_action**\ (\ action\: :ref:`MismatchAction<enum_NetwMultiplayer_MismatchAction>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_mismatch_action>`

Sets what happens when the server's tickrate differs from this peer's. The default is :ref:`MISMATCH_ACTION_WARN<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_MISMATCH_ACTION_WARN>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_param:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **clock_set_param**\ (\ param\: :ref:`ClockParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_ClockParam>`, value\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_param>`

Changes one clock setting. The tickrate can only be set with :ref:`Netw.configure_clock()<class_Netw_method_configure_clock>`.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                     the setting changed
    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED       param was CLOCK_PARAM_TICKRATE
    ┖╴ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER  param is unknown

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_synchronized:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **clock_set_synchronized**\ (\ value\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_set_synchronized>`

Sets :ref:`clock_is_synchronized()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_is_synchronized>`. Set it to ``false`` to resynchronize from scratch on the next ping.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step:

|void| **clock_step**\ (\ count\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_step>`

Runs ``count`` ticks immediately. It does not emit :ref:`clock_before_tick_loop<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick_loop>`. Wrap it in :ref:`clock_tick_loop()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_tick_loop>` when that is needed.

|void| **clock_tick_loop**\ (\ open\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_clock_tick_loop>`

Emits :ref:`clock_before_tick_loop<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_before_tick_loop>` when ``open`` is ``true``, and :ref:`clock_after_tick_loop<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick_loop>` otherwise. Predicted entities only run between the two.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_complete_auth:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_close:

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_close**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_close>`

Closes ``database`` once its pending operations finish. The promise resolves with an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>`, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.close()<class_NetwDatabase_method_close>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_create:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **database_create**\ (\ name\: :godot:`StringName`, config\: :ref:`NetwDatabaseConfig<class_NetwDatabaseConfig>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_create>`

Declares a database named ``name`` from ``config`` and returns its :godot:`RID`. A name already declared returns the :godot:`RID` it has, or an invalid one when ``config`` names a different backend.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_delete_slot:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_delete_slot**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, slot\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_delete_slot>`

Removes ``slot`` and everything stored in it. Can be called before :ref:`database_open()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open>`. Deleting the open slot fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_BUSY <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_BUSY>`. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.delete_slot()<class_NetwDatabase_method_delete_slot>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_erase:

Removes the record at ``id``. Erasing a record that is not there succeeds. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.erase()<class_NetwDatabase_method_erase>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_find:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **database_find**\ (\ name\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_find>`

Resolves once every operation started on ``database`` before this call has finished. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.flush()<class_NetwDatabase_method_flush>` for the errors.

The slot ``database`` holds open, or empty when it is not open.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_list_slots:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_list_slots**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_list_slots>`

Lists every slot stored in ``database``, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.list_slots()<class_NetwDatabase_method_list_slots>`. Can be called before :ref:`database_open()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open>`.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_open**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, slot\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_open>`

Opens ``slot``. Other operations fail until the promise resolves. Opening a different slot while one is open fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_BUSY <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_BUSY>`. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.open()<class_NetwDatabase_method_open>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_patch:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_patch**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, id\: :godot:`StringName`, values\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_patch>`

Writes the fields in ``values`` to the record at ``id`` and keeps the rest. A missing record fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST>`. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.patch()<class_NetwDatabase_method_patch>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_read:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_read**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, id\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_read>`

Reads the record at ``id``, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.read()<class_NetwDatabase_method_read>` for the result. ``schema`` is a sealed schema, such as one from :ref:`schema_find()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_schema_find>`.

A ``database`` that is not open fails the promise with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>`, an unsealed ``schema`` with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST>`, and an empty ``id`` with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_scan:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_scan**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, filter\: :godot:`Dictionary`, cursor\: :godot:`String`, limit\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_scan>`

Reads up to ``limit`` records matching ``filter``, see :ref:`NetwDatabase.scan()<class_NetwDatabase_method_scan>` for the result. Pass an empty ``cursor`` to start, and the cursor of the previous page to continue.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_submit:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_submit**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, operations\: :godot:`Array`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_submit>`

Applies ``operations`` in order, see :ref:`NetwWriteBatch.submit()<class_NetwWriteBatch_method_submit>` for the result. Build ``operations`` with :ref:`NetwWriteBatch<class_NetwWriteBatch>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_write:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **database_write**\ (\ database\: :godot:`RID`, schema\: :godot:`RID`, id\: :godot:`StringName`, values\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_database_write>`

Replaces the whole record at ``id``. ``values`` must hold every column ``schema`` declares and nothing else. See :ref:`NetwDatabase.write()<class_NetwDatabase_method_write>` for the errors.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_set_callback:

|void| **display_set_callback**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, callback\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_set_callback>`

Calls ``callback`` as ``callback(entity, track, value)`` for every displayed value, in place of writing the node. An empty :godot:`Callable` clears it. Clears :ref:`display_set_target_item()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_set_target_item>`.

----

|void| **display_set_target_item**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, item\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_set_target_item>`

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **display_write_default**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, track\: :godot:`StringName`, value\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_display_write_default>`

The default :ref:`_display_write()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__display_write>`. Writes ``value`` to ``track`` on ``entity``.

Replaces :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>` with ``inner`` without restarting the session. An auth callback set on ``inner`` replaces this session's.

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_dispose:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **embed_dispose**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_dispose>`

Shuts the session down and frees everything it owns. Calls queued with :ref:`session_flush_deferred()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred>` run first. Calling it again does nothing.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_is_disposing:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **embed_is_disposing**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_is_disposing>`

Returns ``true`` once :ref:`embed_dispose()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_dispose>` has started. Use it to tell a local shutdown apart from a lost connection.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_offer_bare_level:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **embed_offer_bare_level**\ (\ level\: :godot:`Node`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_offer_bare_level>`

Sets ``level`` as the scene :ref:`embed_settle()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_settle>` starts the session with. Pass ``null`` for none.

When ``level`` is a multiplayer scene, the server spawns it again from its ``.tscn`` file and clients receive it from the server.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_phase:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`EmbedPhase<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EmbedPhase>` **embed_phase**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_phase>`

How far the session has started up. :ref:`embed_settle()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_settle>` moves it to :ref:`EMBED_PHASE_LIVE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EMBED_PHASE_LIVE>`. Being live does not mean connected, which :ref:`state<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_state>` tells.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_poll_transport:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **embed_poll_transport**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_poll_transport>`

Sends and receives pending packets. The session calls it every frame and after every tick. Call it to read the newest packets before running game code. Returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>` when there is no :ref:`inner<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_inner>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_settle:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **embed_settle**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_settle>`

Applies every ``Netw.configure_*`` call made so far and moves :ref:`embed_phase()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_embed_phase>` to :ref:`EMBED_PHASE_LIVE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_EMBED_PHASE_LIVE>`. :ref:`MultiplayerTree<class_MultiplayerTree>` calls it once on the first frame. Later calls do nothing.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                the session is live
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED  the session is live, but a channel has no handler

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_add:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **endpoint_add**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, address\: :godot:`String`, display_name\: :godot:`String` = ""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_add>`

Adds a server at ``address`` reached through ``transport``, and emits :ref:`endpoint_added<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_added>`. Adding the same pair again returns the same :godot:`RID`. Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when ``transport`` is unknown.

::

    var endpoint := api.endpoint_add(
            api.transport_find(&"ENetMultiplayerPeer"),
            "203.0.113.42:21253")

----

Returns the endpoint for ``transport`` and ``address``, or an invalid :godot:`RID`.

----

Returns what the last probe found for ``endpoint``. :ref:`ENDPOINT_STATE_STATUS<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_STATE_STATUS>` is :godot:`@GlobalScope.FAILED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_FAILED>` until the first probe.
