Changes one field of ``endpoint`` and emits :ref:`endpoint_updated<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_endpoint_updated>`. Only :ref:`ENDPOINT_PARAM_DISPLAY_NAME<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_ENDPOINT_PARAM_DISPLAY_NAME>` can be changed.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_add_property_set:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_add_property_set**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, set\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_add_property_set>`

Synchronizes the properties in ``set`` on the node ``comp`` of ``entity``. ``0`` is the entity's root node. Create ``set`` with :ref:`property_set_create()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_create>` and seal it with :ref:`property_set_seal()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_property_set_seal>` first.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the set was attached
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  entity or set does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    the set is not sealed
    ┠╴ERR_UNAVAILABLE     comp is not a node of this entity
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the session has no sync pipeline

Gives ``entity`` a new route, the id peers use to address it, and returns it. An entity that has one returns the same route. Returns ``0`` on a client or for an unknown entity.

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **entity_adopt**\ (\ root\: :godot:`Object`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_adopt>`

Replicates a node that is already in the scene tree, and returns its entity. Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` on failure. Use :ref:`entity_replicate()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_replicate>` for a node that is not in the tree yet.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_node:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_bind_node**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, node\: :godot:`Node`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_node>`

Makes ``node`` the node of ``entity``.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the node was bound, or already was
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  entity does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    node is null, or entity has no route yet
    ┖╴ERR_ALREADY_EXISTS  a different node is already bound

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_route:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_bind_route**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, route\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_route>`

Gives ``entity`` a route received from another peer.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the route was assigned
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  entity does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    route is not positive
    ┖╴ERR_ALREADY_IN_USE  another entity has this route

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_call:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_call**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, comp\: :godot:`int`, method\: :godot:`StringName`, args\: :godot:`Array`, peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_call>`

Calls ``method`` as an RPC on the node ``comp`` of ``entity``. ``peer`` ``0`` sends it to every peer :ref:`rpc_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call>` would. The method needs an ``@rpc`` annotation or a :ref:`Netw.configure_rpc()<class_Netw_method_configure_rpc>` declaration.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the call was sent
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the node or the method does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_UNAVAILABLE     the node is not ready on this peer yet
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the method is not an RPC

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_create:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **entity_create**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_create>`

Creates an empty entity. Give it a route with :ref:`entity_admit()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_admit>` or :ref:`entity_bind_route()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_route>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_describe:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **entity_describe**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_describe>`

Returns what this peer knows about the entity at ``route``, or an empty :godot:`Dictionary`. ``stage`` is a :ref:`Stage<enum_NetwEntity_Stage>` and ``liveness`` is an :ref:`EntityState<enum_NetwMultiplayer_EntityState>`.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴route        int              the route asked about
    ┠╴entity_id    StringName       the durable label
    ┠╴peer_id      int              the peer this entity represents, 0 for a server-owned one
    ┠╴stage        int              the entity's Stage
    ┠╴controller   int              the peer that steers it, 0 for the server
    ┠╴liveness     int              the entity's EntityState
    ┖╴layers       Array            the interest layers it belongs to, in join order

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_despawn:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **entity_despawn**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, opts\: :ref:`NetwDespawnOpts<class_NetwDespawnOpts>` = null\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_despawn>`

Despawns ``entity`` on every peer. To hide it from one peer only, use :ref:`interest_leave()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_leave>`. ``opts`` sets options for this call.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the entity is despawning
    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED    called on a client
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the entity does not exist

\ **Server Only.**

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_arm:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **event_arm**\ (\ enabled\: :godot:`bool`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_arm>`

Records every :ref:`Event<enum_NetwMultiplayer_Event>` for :ref:`event_ring()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_ring>` when ``enabled`` is ``true``. When ``false``, only events an :ref:`event_watch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_watch>` asks for are recorded.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_ring:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Array`\[:godot:`Dictionary`\] **event_ring**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_ring>`

Returns and clears the events recorded for ``route``, oldest first. Route ``0`` holds events that belong to no entity. Each record has the shape shown in :ref:`event_watch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_watch>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_unwatch:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **event_unwatch**\ (\ id\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_unwatch>`

Removes the watch ``id`` returned by :ref:`event_watch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_watch>`. Returns ``false`` when there was none.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_watch:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`int` **event_watch**\ (\ events\: :godot:`PackedInt64Array`, target\: :godot:`Dictionary` = {}, predicate\: :godot:`Dictionary` = {}, sink\: :godot:`Callable` = Callable(), opts\: :godot:`Dictionary` = {}\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_watch>`

Calls ``sink`` with a record every time one of ``events`` happens. Returns an id for :ref:`event_unwatch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_event_unwatch>`.

::

    var id := api.event_watch(
            [NetwMultiplayer.EVENT_DESPAWNED],
            {route = car_route},
            {},
            _on_despawn)

    func _on_despawn(record: Dictionary) -> void:
        print(record.tick, " ", record.verdict)

\ ``target`` filters by entity or peer. ``opts`` accepts ``once``, ``dedupe``, ``phase`` and ``note``.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴route      int         only events about this entity
    ┠╴entity_id  StringName  the same, by entity id
    ┖╴peer       int         only events involving this peer

\ Every record has every key below.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴event       int         one value of Event
    ┠╴event_name  String      the name of that value
    ┠╴phase       int         0 before the event, 1 after it
    ┠╴tick        int         the tick it happened on
    ┠╴route       int         the entity, 0 when none
    ┠╴entity_id   StringName  the entity id, empty if unknown
    ┠╴peer        int         the peer involved, 0 when none
    ┠╴verdict     int         the Error of a decision, OK otherwise
    ┠╴detail      Dictionary  extra values, keys depend on the event
    ┖╴model       Dictionary  the entity's last state, on despawn only

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_flush_now:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **interest_flush_now**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_flush_now>`

Applies pending layer changes now, where they would otherwise apply at the end of the frame.

::

    layer.add_viewer(peer)
    api.interest_flush_now()
    assert(api.interest_admits(entity, peer))

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the changes were applied
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    a driver callback did not return an Array
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  a driver callback returned an unknown entity

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

|void| **interest_on_enter**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, layer_id\: :godot:`StringName`, callback\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_enter>`

Calls ``callback`` as ``callback(layer_id, peer_id)`` when a peer starts seeing ``entity`` through ``layer_id``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_leave:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **interest_on_leave**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, layer_id\: :godot:`StringName`, callback\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_leave>`

Calls ``callback`` as ``callback(layer_id, peer_id)`` when a peer stops seeing ``entity`` through ``layer_id``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_leave_policy:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **interest_on_leave_policy**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, layer_id\: :godot:`StringName`, policy\: :ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>`, custom_callback\: :godot:`Callable` = Callable()\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_leave_policy>`

Sets the :ref:`LeavePolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_LeavePolicy>` for ``entity`` on ``layer_id``. :ref:`LEAVE_POLICY_CUSTOM<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_LEAVE_POLICY_CUSTOM>` requires ``custom_callback``, called with ``(peer_id, layer_id)`` on the server. Other policies reject a callback.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_perception_policy:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **interest_on_perception_policy**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, layer_id\: :godot:`StringName`, policy\: :ref:`PerceptionPolicy<enum_NetwMultiplayer_PerceptionPolicy>`, custom_callback\: :godot:`Callable` = Callable()\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_on_perception_policy>`

Sets what this peer does with ``entity`` when its visibility through ``layer_id`` changes. :ref:`PERCEPTION_POLICY_CUSTOM<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_PERCEPTION_POLICY_CUSTOM>` requires ``custom_callback``, called as ``custom_callback(visible, peer_id, layer_id)``.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_player_sees:

:godot:`bool` **interest_player_sees**\ (\ peer_id\: :godot:`int`, entity\: :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_player_sees>`

Returns ``true`` when the player ``peer_id`` can see ``entity``. A host player follows the same rules as a client. A client can only ask about itself.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

Returns every other entity that shares the layer ``layer_id`` with ``entity``, sorted by :ref:`NetwEntity.entity_id<class_NetwEntity_property_entity_id>`. An empty ``layer_id`` checks every layer of ``entity``.

Returns ``true`` when updates for ``entity`` are sent to ``peer_id``. Always ``true`` for the server, and :ref:`interest_player_sees()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_player_sees>` for everyone else.

Starts a predicted effect ``key``. ``revert`` runs if the server denies it or does not confirm it within ``timeout_ticks``. ``0`` uses the default timeout.

Denies the effect ``key``, runs its revert, and calls the ``denied`` callbacks from :ref:`lagcomp_effect_watch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_effect_watch>`.

Calls ``confirmed`` or ``denied`` when the effect ``key`` resolves. Returns ``false`` when the effect is not pending.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_is_configured:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`bool` **lagcomp_is_configured**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_is_configured>`

Returns ``true`` when a :ref:`NetwLagCompensationConfig<class_NetwLagCompensationConfig>` was applied. Without one, rewinding does nothing.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_metrics:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **lagcomp_metrics**\ (\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_metrics>`

Returns lag compensation counters. Totals count from startup.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴entities          int         entities simulated this tick
    ┠╴timelines         int         rewindable entities recorded this tick
    ┠╴corrections       int         total corrections
    ┠╴max_replay_depth  int         most ticks replayed at once
    ┠╴consumed          int         total inputs the server used
    ┠╴missing           int         total input ticks that never arrived
    ┠╴folded            int         physics frames merged into an existing input
    ┠╴joint             Dictionary  totals for bodies simulated together
    ┠╴pending_actions   int         actions waiting to run
    ┠╴effects_armed     int         effects waiting to be confirmed or denied
    ┖╴gate_fallbacks    int         actions that ran without all their state

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_rewind:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **lagcomp_rewind**\ (\ entities\: :godot:`Array`\[:godot:`RID`\], tick\: :godot:`int`, body\: :godot:`Callable`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_rewind>`

Moves every entity in ``entities`` back to where it was at ``tick``, calls ``body``, then moves them back. Entities with no history at ``tick`` are skipped. :ref:`Netw.rewind()<class_Netw_method_rewind>` does the same with :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` values.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_sample:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`DictionaryRecord<class_DictionaryRecord>` **lagcomp_sample**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`, tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_sample>`

Returns a copy of the state of ``entity`` at ``tick``. The record is empty when nothing was recorded.

::

    var past := api.lagcomp_sample(entity, tick)
    if past.has_value(&"position"):
        print(past.position)

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_timeline_declare:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **lagcomp_timeline_declare**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_timeline_declare>`

Starts recording the history of ``entity`` so it can be rewound. The server already does this for entities with synchronized state.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  recording started
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the entity has no node

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_lagcomp_timeline_of:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

Returns the recorded history of ``entity`` used for rewinding, or ``null``. This is the authority's history. :ref:`NetwEntity.timeline<class_NetwEntity_property_timeline>` is what this peer predicted.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_get_bucket:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Variant` **peer_get_bucket**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, bucket_type\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_get_bucket>`

Returns an instance of ``bucket_type`` stored for ``peer``, created on first use. Use it to keep game data per peer.

::

    class Bucket extends RefCounted:
        var ready := false

    var bucket := api.peer_get_bucket(peer, Bucket) as Bucket

\ ``bucket_type`` must create a :godot:`RefCounted`. Returns ``null`` for an unknown peer. :ref:`peer_forget()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_forget>` deletes the bucket.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

Disconnects ``peer_id``. When ``reason`` is not empty, that peer receives :ref:`peer_kicked<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_peer_kicked>` first. Does nothing on a client.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_link_stats:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **peer_link_stats**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_peer_link_stats>`

Returns measured connection quality to ``peer``.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴loss          float   share of the last 32 packets lost
    ┠╴rtt           float   round trip time, in seconds
    ┠╴jitter        float   round trip variation, in seconds
    ┠╴reorders      int     packets that arrived out of order
    ┠╴duplicates    int     packets that arrived twice
    ┠╴mode          String  "good" or "bad"
    ┖╴budget_bits   int     most bits one packet to this peer may use

\ On a ``bad`` connection, ``budget_bits`` is halved to send less.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_persist_tick_default:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **persist_tick_default**\ (\ delta\: :godot:`float`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_persist_tick_default>`

The default :ref:`_persist_tick()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__persist_tick>`. Saves every changed entity whose :ref:`NetwPersistenceConfig.interval()<class_NetwPersistenceConfig_method_interval>` has passed. Does nothing on a client.

::

    extends NetwMultiplayer

    func _persist_tick(delta: float) -> void:
        if not saving_paused:
            persist_tick_default(delta)

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **rpc_call**\ (\ callable\: :godot:`Callable`, args\: :godot:`Array`, peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call>`

Calls ``callable`` with ``args`` as an RPC on ``peer``, or on every peer from :ref:`rpc_get_recipients()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_get_recipients>` when ``peer`` is ``0``. The node must belong to an entity, and the method must be an RPC. A :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>` in ``args`` arrives as the receiver's own :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_channel_register:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **rpc_channel_register**\ (\ channel\: :godot:`int`, handler\: :godot:`Callable`, defer_when_unknown\: :godot:`bool` = false\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_channel_register>`

Calls ``handler`` as ``handler(entity, payload, sender)`` for every packet on ``channel``, which must be between 100 and 254. When ``defer_when_unknown`` is ``true``, a packet for an entity that is not spawned yet waits for it.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_get_recipients:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`PackedInt32Array` **rpc_get_recipients**\ (\ entity\: :ref:`NetwEntity<class_NetwEntity>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_get_recipients>`

Returns the peers an RPC on ``entity`` is sent to, which is every connected peer except this one. Offline, it returns ``[1]``. Use :ref:`interest_player_sees()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_player_sees>` to ask who can see the entity.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **rpc_request_call**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, callable\: :godot:`Callable`, args\: :godot:`Array`, timeout_seconds\: :godot:`float` = 5.0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call>`

Calls ``callable`` on ``peer`` and returns a :ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` that resolves with its return value. The promise is rejected after ``timeout_seconds``. Returns ``null`` when :ref:`rpc_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_call>` would reject the call.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call_group:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwGroupPromise<class_NetwGroupPromise>` **rpc_request_call_group**\ (\ callable\: :godot:`Callable`, args\: :godot:`Array`, timeout_seconds\: :godot:`float` = 5.0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call_group>`

Calls ``callable`` on every peer that sees its entity, and returns a :ref:`NetwGroupPromise<class_NetwGroupPromise>` of their replies. A peer that disconnects counts as rejected. Returns ``null`` when :ref:`rpc_request_call()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_request_call>` would.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

|void| **rpc_send_reply**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, route\: :godot:`int`, txn\: :godot:`int`, value\: :godot:`Variant`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_rpc_send_reply>`

Replies ``value`` to request ``txn`` from ``peer``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_change_to_file:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **scene_change_to_file**\ (\ requester\: :godot:`Node`, path\: :godot:`String`, scope\: :ref:`SceneChange<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange>` = 0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_change_to_file>`

Opens the file-backed scene at ``path`` and makes players watch it. No entity moves, so spawn the arrivals from :ref:`scene_changed<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_scene_changed>`.

\ ``scope`` says who moves. ``requester`` is the node asking, which picks the player for :ref:`SCENE_CHANGE_PLAYER<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_CHANGE_PLAYER>` and the scene for :ref:`SCENE_CHANGE_SCENE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_CHANGE_SCENE>`. On a client it sends a :ref:`scene_request()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_request>` to the server.

::

    var promise := session.scene_change_to_file(self, "res://arena.tscn")
    await promise.wait()

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_change_to_packed:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **scene_change_to_packed**\ (\ requester\: :godot:`Node`, packed\: :godot:`PackedScene`, scope\: :ref:`SceneChange<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneChange>` = 0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_change_to_packed>`

Like :ref:`scene_change_to_file()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_change_to_file>`, taking a :godot:`PackedScene` as :godot:`SceneTree.change_scene_to_packed() <SceneTree#class_SceneTree_method_change_scene_to_packed>` does. ``packed`` must be saved to a file, or the promise fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNAVAILABLE <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNAVAILABLE>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_create:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **scene_create**\ (\ recipe\: :godot:`Variant`, isolation\: :ref:`SceneIsolation<enum_NetwMultiplayer_SceneIsolation>` = 0\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_create>`

Spawns a multiplayer scene from ``recipe`` and returns it, or an invalid :godot:`RID` on failure. ``null`` creates an empty scene. ``isolation`` decides whether it has its own physics and rendering world.

\ **Server Only.**

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_declare:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **scene_declare**\ (\ entity\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_declare>`

Makes ``entity`` a multiplayer scene that replicates its nodes to the players in it. Call it before :ref:`entity_bind_node()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_entity_bind_node>`. A client can only declare an entity that has a route from the server. :ref:`scene_undeclare()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_undeclare>` reverses it.

::

    var mirror := api.entity_create()
    api.entity_bind_route(mirror, route) # the server's route
    api.scene_declare(mirror)

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the entity is a scene
    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED    called on a client without the entity's route
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the entity does not exist
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the entity already has a node

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_observe:

Calls ``callback`` when ``event`` happens on ``scene``. Adding the same callback twice has no effect. Return ``true`` from ``callback`` to stop receiving events. :ref:`scene_unobserve()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_unobserve>` removes it.

On a host, returns the :godot:`SubViewport` of the scene from :ref:`scene_presented()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_presented>`, or ``null``. Only scenes with :ref:`SCENE_ISOLATION_OWN_WORLD<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SCENE_ISOLATION_OWN_WORLD>` have one. :ref:`participant_viewport_changed<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_participant_viewport_changed>` emits when it changes. :ref:`HostSceneView<class_HostSceneView>` displays it.

Reloads the displayed scene, like :godot:`SceneTree.reload_current_scene() <SceneTree#class_SceneTree_method_reload_current_scene>`. Fails with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNAVAILABLE <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNAVAILABLE>` when there is no scene or it has no file.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_request:

Asks the server to move the local player to the scene file at ``path``.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED  the server refused
    ┠╴ERR_SKIP          a newer request replaced this one
    ┖╴ERR_TIMEOUT       no answer within ten seconds

\ **Player request.**

Replaces the :ref:`HostSceneView<class_HostSceneView>` a host creates to display its player's scene. ``factory`` is ``func(root: Node) -> Node``. Returning ``null`` creates nothing. No view is created when a :ref:`ParticipantView<class_ParticipantView>` already exists.

Reverses :ref:`scene_watch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_watch>`. A peer with a body in the scene keeps receiving it. Returns ``false`` when the scene does not exist.

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_watch:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **scene_watch**\ (\ scene\: :godot:`RID`, peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_watch>`

Replicates ``scene`` to ``peer`` until :ref:`scene_unwatch()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_scene_unwatch>`, without moving any of its bodies. Applies immediately.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                     the peer receives the scene
    ┠╴ERR_UNAUTHORIZED       called on a client
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER  peer is 0
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST     the scene has no node
    ┖╴ERR_UNAVAILABLE        the peer still does not receive it

\ **Server Only.**

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

Adds the column ``key`` to ``schema`` and returns its index. ``stride`` is how many values each row holds. Returns ``-1`` for a duplicate key, an invalid type or stride, or a sealed schema.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_to:

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **send_to**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`, route\: :godot:`int`, channel\: :godot:`int`, payload\: :godot:`PackedByteArray`, reliable\: :godot:`bool`, comp\: :godot:`int` = 0, path\: :godot:`String` = "", batched\: :godot:`bool` = false\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_send_to>`

Sends ``payload`` on ``channel`` to ``peer``, about the entity at ``route``. A payload for this peer is handled locally. When a clock runs, some channels wait and send together at the next tick.

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                     the payload was sent or queued
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER  route is negative
    ┖╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED       the session cannot send yet

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_answer_probe**\ (\ peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_answer_probe>`

Answers a server browser probe from ``peer`` with the info from :ref:`Netw.configure_server_info()<class_Netw_method_configure_server_info>`, or :ref:`NetwServerInfo.from_session()<class_NetwServerInfo_method_from_session>` by default. .. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_cancel_deferred:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_cancel_deferred**\ (\ key\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_cancel_deferred>`

Removes the call queued under ``key`` by :ref:`session_defer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer>`.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_defer**\ (\ fn\: :godot:`Callable`, key\: :godot:`StringName` = &""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer>`

Queues ``fn`` to run at the next :ref:`session_flush_deferred()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred>`. A ``key`` already queued moves to the back and runs once. Calls queued while flushing run in the same flush.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_flush_deferred**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_deferred>`

Runs every call queued with :ref:`session_defer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_defer>` until none are left. This is where the session reacts to nodes added, moved or freed. The session calls it every frame. Call it to see the result of tree changes before then.

Do not call it from :godot:`Node.tree_entered <Node#class_Node_signal_tree_entered>` or :godot:`Node.tree_exiting <Node#class_Node_signal_tree_exiting>`, since a :godot:`Node.reparent() <Node#class_Node_method_reparent>` may be half done. An error is pushed if calls keep queueing themselves forever.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_tick:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **session_flush_tick**\ (\ tick\: :godot:`int`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_flush_tick>`

Sends everything for ``tick``. The session calls it on :ref:`clock_after_tick<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_clock_after_tick>`, before any game listener. Call it only when driving ticks without the clock. Returns the error of :ref:`interest_flush_now()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_interest_flush_now>` when that fails.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

The role the game asked for in :ref:`NetwSessionConfig.desired_role<class_NetwSessionConfig_property_desired_role>`. :ref:`role<class_NetwMultiplayer_property_role>` is the role the session actually has.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_leave:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **session_leave**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_leave>`

Disconnects and returns to :ref:`SESSION_STATE_OFFLINE<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_SESSION_STATE_OFFLINE>`. On the server, it saves every persistent entity first, and a failed save keeps the session online. The promise resolves with an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` within three seconds.

::

    var left := api.session_leave()
    if not left.is_settled:
        await left.wait()
    if left.result != OK:
        push_error("still online: %s" % error_string(left.result))

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_notify_shutdown:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_notify_shutdown**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String` = ""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_notify_shutdown>`

Emits :ref:`session_server_disconnecting<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_server_disconnecting>` with ``reason`` on every peer. It does not disconnect anyone.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

|void| **session_pause**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String` = ""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_pause>`

Pauses the game on every peer, which emits :ref:`session_tree_paused<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_tree_paused>` with ``reason``.

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_prepare_join:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **session_prepare_join**\ (\ username\: :godot:`StringName`, args\: :godot:`Array` = []\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_prepare_join>`

Sets the join request sent when :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.multiplayer_peer <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_property_multiplayer_peer>` connects. A server sends it at :ref:`session_submit_join()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_submit_join>`. The promise result is an :godot:`@GlobalScope.Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>`.

::

    var prepared := api.session_prepare_join(&"Dev")
    if not prepared.is_settled:
        await prepared.wait()
    if int(prepared.result) != OK:
        return

\ An empty ``username`` returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER>`.

\ **Player request.**

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_request_leave:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **session_request_leave**\ (\ reason\: :godot:`String` = ""\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_session_request_leave>`

Asks the server to leave. The server receives :ref:`session_disconnect_requested<class_NetwMultiplayer_signal_session_disconnect_requested>` with ``reason`` and decides.

\ **Player request.**

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

The default :ref:`_sync_apply_set()<class_NetwMultiplayer_private_method__sync_apply_set>`. Returns :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNCONFIGURED <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNCONFIGURED>` when called outside of receiving a packet.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_sync_explain:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Dictionary` **sync_explain**\ (\ route\: :godot:`int`, comp\: :godot:`int`, peer\: :godot:`int`\ ) |const| :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_sync_explain>`

Explains why the properties of node ``comp`` on the entity at ``route`` were or were not sent to ``peer`` last time. For debugging, not for every frame.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴verdict       String  one of the values below
    ┠╴tick          int     the tick of the last send
    ┠╴confirmed     int     the newest version the peer confirmed
    ┠╴exposed       int     the newest version sent
    ┖╴has_baseline  bool    whether only changes can be sent

\ ``verdict`` is ``"sent"``, ``"deferred"`` when there was no bandwidth left, ``"caught_up"`` when the peer already had everything, ``"refused"`` when writing it failed, ``"ungathered"`` when nothing was read, or ``"unoffered"`` when never sent to this peer.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_save:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:ref:`NetwPromise<class_NetwPromise>` **table_save**\ (\ table\: :godot:`RID`, database\: :godot:`RID`, key\: :godot:`StringName`, ids\: :godot:`PackedStringArray`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_save>`

Saves the rows of ``table`` under ``key`` in ``database``. ``ids`` names each row, in the order of :ref:`table_read_routes()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_read_routes>`. See :ref:`NetwTableHandle.save()<class_NetwTableHandle_method_save>` for the errors.

\ **Server Only.**

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_write_column:

Writes ``data`` to ``column`` of ``table``. It applies at :ref:`table_commit()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_table_commit>`.

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the column was written
    ┠╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  the table does not exist
    ┠╴ERR_UNCONFIGURED    the schema is not sealed
    ┖╴ERR_INVALID_DATA    unknown column, or data has the wrong type

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_cancel_peer_creation:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

|void| **transport_cancel_peer_creation**\ (\ ticket\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_cancel_peer_creation>`

Cancels the :ref:`transport_create_peer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>` request ``ticket``. Its callback receives ``(null, ERR_SKIP, "")``. Does nothing once the callback has started.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **transport_create_peer**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, mode\: :ref:`TransportMode<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportMode>`, address\: :godot:`String`, settings\: :godot:`Dictionary`, completed\: :godot:`Callable`, progress\: :godot:`Callable` = Callable()\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>`

Asks ``transport`` to create a :godot:`MultiplayerPeer` and returns a ticket for :ref:`transport_cancel_peer_creation()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_cancel_peer_creation>`. ``address`` is used by clients and ``settings`` by hosts.

\ ``completed`` is called once, in a later frame, as ``completed(peer, error, detail)``. Assign the peer to :godot:`MultiplayerAPI.multiplayer_peer <MultiplayerAPI#class_MultiplayerAPI_property_multiplayer_peer>` inside this callback, before any ``await``. Returning without assigning it closes the peer. ``progress`` is called as ``progress(step, message, ratio)`` while it works.

Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when ``completed`` is not valid.

::

    var ticket := api.transport_create_peer(
            relay, NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_MODE_CLIENT,
            "eu-1.example.com", {}, _created)

    func _created(peer: MultiplayerPeer, error: Error, _detail: String):
        if error != OK:
            return
        api.multiplayer_peer = peer

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_find:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **transport_find**\ (\ peer_class\: :godot:`StringName`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_find>`

Returns the transport that creates ``peer_class`` peers, or an invalid :godot:`RID`.

::

    var enet := api.transport_find(&"ENetMultiplayerPeer")
    api.transport_create_peer(
            enet, NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_MODE_HOST, "",
            {port = 21253}, assign_it)

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_get_param:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Variant` **transport_get_param**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, param\: :ref:`TransportParam<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportParam>`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_get_param>`

Returns one field of ``transport``, used to draw host and join forms, or ``null``. :ref:`TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES<class_NetwMultiplayer_constant_TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES>` returns a :ref:`TransportCapability<enum_NetwMultiplayer_TransportCapability>` mask.

::

    var caps: int = api.transport_get_param(
            t, NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES)
    if caps & NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_CAN_HOST:
        show_host_button()

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_list:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Array` **transport_list**\ (\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_list>`

Every available transport, the registered ones first, then those from a :ref:`LobbyDirectory<class_LobbyDirectory>`. Any other :godot:`MultiplayerPeer` still works when assigned directly.

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_register:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`RID` **transport_register**\ (\ type\: :godot:`Script`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_register>`

Registers the :ref:`NetwTransport<class_NetwTransport>` script ``type`` with this session. Registering it again returns the same :godot:`RID`. Returns an invalid :godot:`RID` when another script is already registered for the same peer class. It replaces the built-in transport of that class.

::

    func _ready() -> void:
        var api := Netw.of(self)
        var relay := api.transport_register(MyRelayTransport)
        var caps: int = api.transport_get_param(
                relay, NetwMultiplayer.TRANSPORT_PARAM_CAPABILITIES)

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_set_browse_settings:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **transport_set_browse_settings**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`, settings\: :godot:`Dictionary`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_set_browse_settings>`

Sets how ``transport`` looks for servers. Call it before :ref:`endpoint_refresh()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_endpoint_refresh>`. The WebRTC transport accepts these settings.

.. code:: text

    Dictionary
    ┠╴trackers             PackedStringArray  WebSocket tracker URLs
    ┖╴signaling_namespace  String             namespace of listed rooms

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                     the settings are set
    ┠╴ERR_INVALID_PARAMETER  invalid settings
    ┖╴ERR_ALREADY_IN_USE     different settings after searching started

.. rst-class:: classref-item-separator

----

.. _class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_unregister:

.. rst-class:: classref-method

:godot:`Error <@GlobalScope#enum_@globalscope_Error>` **transport_unregister**\ (\ transport\: :godot:`RID`\ ) :ref:`🔗<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_unregister>`

Reverses :ref:`transport_register()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_register>`. A peer it already created stays connected. Pending :ref:`transport_create_peer()<class_NetwMultiplayer_method_transport_create_peer>` requests fail with :godot:`@GlobalScope.ERR_UNAVAILABLE <@GlobalScope#class_@GlobalScope_constant_ERR_UNAVAILABLE>`.

::

    var relay := api.transport_register(MyRelayTransport)
    # ... later, when the relay SDK is shut down
    api.transport_unregister(relay)

.. code:: text

    Error
    ┠╴OK                  the transport was removed
    ┖╴ERR_DOES_NOT_EXIST  not registered on this session
